
local f15147 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    return function(I, C, n, x)
      if type(I) ~= "function" then
        return nil
      elseif n[I] then
        return nil
      elseif x < 0 then
        return nil
      end
      n[I] = true
      if debug.info(I, "n") == C then
        return I
      end
      if type(islclosure) == "function" then
        if not (islclosure(I)) then
          return nil
        end
      end
      local h, Y = pcall(debug.getupvalues, I)
      if h then
        if type(Y) ~= "table" then
          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      for h, h in Y, nil, nil do
        I = D.Search(h, C, n, x - 1)
        if I then
          return I
        end
      end
      return nil
    end
  end
end

local f13558 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    return function()
      if D.CarryCount() > 0 then
        C.Fire("DropCarriedRemote")
        local I = os.clock() + 0.6
        while true do
          local C = ((D.CarryCount() > 0) and (os.clock() < I)) and not n.Unloaded
          if not C then
            break
          end
          x.WaitKick(0.1)
        end
      end
      x.Decoy = nil
    end
  end
end

local f10887 = function(I, I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    return function(I, h)
      if D.Flags.CollectDrops ~= true then
        return false
      elseif h.Hatch ~= nil then
        return false
      end
      local D, h = I:GetPivot().Position, C.Home()
      if h ~= nil then
        if (D - h.Position).Magnitude <= 100 then
          return true
        end
      end
      h = n.Path(x, "Build", "ZoneHitboxes", "SafeZone")
      if h then
        if not (h:IsA("BasePart")) then
          return false
        end
      else
        return false
      end
      I = h.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(D)
      return ((I.X < (-h.Size.X * 0.5)) and (I.X > ((-h.Size.X * 0.5) - 40))) and (math.abs(I.Z) <= 60)
    end
  end
end

local f4099 = function(I, I, I, I)

      end

          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      if type(debug) == "table" then
        if not (type(debug.getupvalues) == "function") then
          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      local C, n = pcall(debug.getupvalues, I.Attach)
      if C then
        if type(n) ~= "table" then
          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      for I, I in n, nil, nil do
        if type(I) == "table" then
          D.Registry = I
          return I
        end
      end
      return nil
    end
  end
end

          pcall(x.Destroy, x)
        end
        D.Badges[I] = nil
        return
      end
      if x then
      else
        x = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        x.Name = "BigFrootInv"
        x.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        x.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        x.BorderSizePixel = 0

        x.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.86, 0.2)

        x.TextScaled = true
        x.ZIndex = 50
        local h = Instance.new("UICorner")
        h.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.3, 0)
        h.Parent = x
        x.Parent = I
        D.Badges[I] = x
      end
      I = "$" .. (C.Short(n) .. "/s")
      if x.Text ~= I then
        x.Text = I
      end
      x.TextColor3 = D.ValueColour(n)
    end
  end
end

local f12097 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    local b = I[10]
    return function()
      local I = D.PickSet("BreakEggs")
      local K = if I then not I else C.HuntZone() or n.FuseZone()
      local C, O, L, W, e, G = K or nil, x.Flags.TargetPriority, h.Damage() / h.HitGap(), os.clock(), (Y.On() and Y.Picks()) or nil, x.Flags.TitanicFirst == true
      if n.FuseOnly() then
        I, C, e, G = nil, n.FuseZone(), nil, false
        if not (C) then
          return nil
        end
      else
      end
      local n, x, h, m, d = {}, {}, -math.huge, math.huge, j.Live
      K = nil
      local z
      for i in d, nil, nil do
        local o = j.Skip[i]
        if j.Alive(i) then
          if o and (o > W) then
          else
            if V.ZoneWalkable(i:GetAttribute("ZoneIndex")) then
              local V, o, r = D.Number(i:GetAttribute("Health"), math.huge) / L, J.Luck(i), (e and Y.Match(i, e)) or (G and i:GetAttribute("Titanic"))
              if r then
                j.Rank(n, i, j.Key(i, V, o, O), o, V)
              end
              if I or C then
                r = (I and I[i:GetAttribute("EggType") or ""]) or (C and (i:GetAttribute("ZoneIndex") == C))
                if r then
                  j.Rank(x, i, j.Key(i, V, o, O), o, V)
                end
              else
                if V < m then
                  m, K = V, i
                else
                end
                if V <= j.AutoCap then
                  local I = ((((D.Number(i:GetAttribute("ZoneIndex"), 1) * 1000) + ((i:GetAttribute("Titanic") and 500) or 0)) + math.min(o, 400)) - V) - (b.Distance(i.Position) / 50)
                  if I > h then
                    h, z = I, i
                  else
                  end
                else
                end
              end
            else
            end
          end
        else
        end
      end
      d = (((n.Egg or j.CyclePick(L, O, W)) or x.Egg) or z) or K
      return d
    end
  end
end

local f12339 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    return function()
      local I = D.Remote("CatchFlingRemote")
      if I then
        if type(getconnections) ~= "function" then
          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      local D, n = pcall(getconnections, I.OnClientEvent)
      if D then
        if type(n) ~= "table" then
          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      for x, x in n, nil, nil do
        D, I = pcall(C.Read, x, "Function")
        if D then
          if type(I) == "function" then
            x = debug.info(I, "s")
            if type(x) == "string" then
              if x:find("CatchFlingController", 1, true) then
                return I
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      return nil
    end
  end
end

local f8938 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    return function(I, Y, j)
      local V = D.PickSet("CycleEggs")
      if C.Flags.EggCycle ~= true then
        n.CycleNote = ""
        return nil
      elseif V then
      else
        n.CycleNote = ""
        return nil
      end
      local C = {}
      for J in V, nil, nil do
        local b = n.ZoneOfType[J]
        if b then
          C[b] = true
        end
      end
      local J, b, K, O, L = {}, {}, n.Live, 0, 0
      for W in K, nil, nil do
        local K, e = n.Skip[W], W:GetAttribute("ZoneIndex")
        if C[e] then
          if n.Alive(W) then
            if K and (K > j) then
            else
              if x.ZoneWalkable(e) then
                local C, x = D.Number(W:GetAttribute("Health"), math.huge) / I, h.Luck(W)
                if V[W:GetAttribute("EggType") or ""] then
                  local I = O + 1
                  n.Rank(J, W, n.Key(W, C, x, Y), x, C)
                  O = I
                else
                  local I = L + 1
                  n.Rank(b, W, n.Key(W, C, x, Y), x, C)
                  L = I
                end
              else
              end
            end
          else
          end
        else
        end
      end
      if n.CyclePhase == "picked" then
        if O == 0 then
          n.CyclePhase = "rest"
        end
      end
      if n.CyclePhase == "rest" then
        if L == 0 then
          n.CyclePhase = "picked"
        end
      end
      n.CycleNote = ("Egg cycle: %s (%d picked, %d others left)"):format(((n.CyclePhase == "picked") and "breaking the picked eggs") or "clearing the others", O, L)
      if n.CyclePhase == "picked" then
        return J.Egg
      end
      return b.Egg
    end
  end
end

local f13627 = function(I, I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    local b = I[10]
    local K = I[11]
    local O = I[12]
    local L = I[13]
    return function(I)
      D.Egg = I
      local W, e = C.Label(I), n.Short(x.Luck(I))
      D.Target = ("%s x%s"):format(W, e)
      if C.Reach(I) > 5 then
        D.Note = "going to " .. W
        if not (h.Go(CFrame.new(C.Stand(I)), "egg")) then
          D.Egg = nil
          return
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
      end
      h.Pickaxe()
      if not (D.Swing(true)) then
        D.Note = "the game's Auto Swing is missing"
        D.Egg = nil
        Y[4][Y[6]]:Later("move", 10)
        return
      end
      local x = j.HitGap()
      local Y, G, m = j.Damage() / x, n.Number(I:GetAttribute("Health"), 0), os.clock()
      D.Interrupt = false
      while true do
        x = (D.On() or V.Running) and not J.Unloaded
        if not x then
          break
        end
        if C.Alive(I) then
          if h.Alive() then
            if j.CarryCount() > 0 then
              break
            end
          else
            break
          end
        else
          break
        end
        local j = n.Number(I:GetAttribute("Health"), 0)
        m = if j < G then os.clock() else m
        D.Note = ("breaking %s x%s  %s hp, ~%s"):format(W, e, n.Short(j), n.Clock((j / Y) + 0.5))
        if D.Repick then
          D.Repick = false
          if not (V.Running) then
            if C.Pick() ~= I then
              D.Note = "settings changed, switching egg"
              G = j
              break
            end
          end
        end
        if D.Interrupt then
          D.Interrupt = false
          if not (V.Running) then
            if (j / Y) > 3 then
              if D.Best(true) then
                G = j
                break
              end
            end
          end
        end
        if D.WindowClosing() then
          if not (V.Running) then
            if D.DecoyPick() then
              D.Note = "window closing: decoy"
              G = j
              break
            end
          end
        end
        if (os.clock() - m) > 8 then
          C.Skip[I] = os.clock() + 60
          b("hits not landing on " .. (W .. ", skipped"))
          G = j
          break
        end
        if C.Reach(I) > 7 then
          h.Go(CFrame.new(C.Stand(I)), "egg")
        end
        if not (K.Character:FindFirstChild("Pickaxe")) then
          h.Pickaxe()
        end
        D.Swing(true)
        task.wait(0.1)
        G = j
      end
      D.Swing(false)
      D.Egg = nil
      if (I:GetAttribute("Hatching") == true) and (I:GetAttribute("HatchBreakerUserId") == K.UserId) then
        Y = os.clock() + 1
        while true do
          x = ((I:GetAttribute("HatchAnimal") == nil) and (os.clock() < Y)) and not J.Unloaded
          if not x then
            break
          end
          D.WaitKick(0.1)
        end
        C.Track(I)
        W = C.Pending[I:GetAttribute("HatchId")]
        e = (((W and not W.Hop) and not O.Wants(W.Name)) and (J.Flags.WaitHatch ~= true)) and L.Why(W)
        if e then
          D.Note = ("left %s ($%s/s): %s"):format(W.Name, n.Short(W.Value), e)
          b(D.Note)
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

        x.MillNote = "loading"
        return
      end
      if D:GetAttribute("TreadmillUnlocked") ~= true then
        if h.Cash() < 100 then
          Y.Set(100, j[4][j[6]], "treadmill")
          x.MillNote = "locked, unlock at $100"
          return

        n.Fire("UnlockTreadmillRequest")
        V.WaitSignal(x.Kick, 3)
      end
      local C = V.Number(D:GetAttribute("TreadmillLevel"), 1)
      local O = V.Call(K.UpgradeCost, C)
      if type(O) ~= "number" then
        x.MillNote = "max level"
        return
      end
      local L, W = V.Call(K.DisplayNameFor, C + 1) or ("level " .. (C + 1)), x.Refused.mill
      local e = (h.Cash() < O) or (W and (W > os.clock()))
      if e then
        Y.Set(O, j[4][j[6]], "treadmill")
        x.MillNote = ("next: %s at $%s"):format(L, V.Short(O))
        return
      end
      n.Fire("UpgradeTreadmillRequest")
      V.WaitSignal(x.Kick, 3)
      if V.Number(D:GetAttribute("TreadmillLevel"), 1) > C then
        J("treadmill upgraded: " .. L)
        x.MillNote = "upgraded to " .. L
        local D, n, h, Y, J, G = b.Push, {Kind = "upgrade", Title = L, Text = "**Upgraded** your treadmill"}, {}, {}, V.Short, V.Call

        e, W = {}, V.Short(O)

        I:lL(h, 0, Y, e)
        n.Extra = h
        D(n)
        j[4][j[6]]:Wake("treadmill", 1)
      else
        x.Refused.mill = os.clock() + 60
        x.MillNote = "upgrade refused"
        j[4][j[6]]:Later("treadmill", 60)
      end
    end
  end
end

local f12971 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    return function(I)
      local x = D.Flags
      if I.Kind == "test" then
        return true
      end
      if I.Kind == "kick" then
        return x.WhKick ~= false
      end
      if I.Kind == "full" then
        return x.WhFull ~= false
      end
      if I.Kind == "upgrade" then
        return x.WhUpgrades == true
      end
      if (I.Kind == "merge") or (I.Kind == "hatch") then
        local D = (x.WhMerge ~= false) and ((I.Value or 0) >= C.Amount(x.WhMinMerge))
        return D
      end
      local D = C.PickSet("WhAnimals")
      if D then
        if not (D[I.Name or ""]) then
          return false
        end
      end
      D = C.PickSet("WhRarities")
      if D then
        if not (D[I.Rarity or ""]) then
          return false
        end
      end
      D = C.PickSet("WhMutations")
      if D then
        local h, Y = n.Trait(I)
        if not ((h and D[h]) or (Y and D[Y])) then
          if h == nil then
            if Y == nil then
              if not (D.Normal) then
                return false
              end
            else
              return false
            end
          else
            return false
          end
        end
      end
      D = C.PickSet("WhSizes")
      if D then
        if not (D[n.SizeOf[I.Bracket or ""] or "Normal"]) then
          return false
        end
      end
      if (I.Value or 0) < C.Amount(x.WhMinRate) then
        return false
      end
      D = C.Amount(x.WhMinKg)
      if D > 0 then
        if I.Kg == nil then
          return false
        elseif I.Kg < D then
          return false
        end
      end
      return true
    end
  end
end

local f11646 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    return function(I)
      local n = {}
      for x, h in I, nil, nil do
        if h.Inst then
          x = n[h.Name] or {Variants = {}, Normals = {}}
          n[h.Name] = x
          table.insert((h.Variant and x.Variants) or x.Normals, h)
        end
      end
      I = {}
      for x, h in n, nil, nil do
        if ((#h.Normals >= 2) and (#h.Variants > 0)) and ((D.Refused[x] or 0) < os.clock()) then
          table.sort(h.Normals, function(D, n)
            return D.Kg > n.Kg
          end)
          local D, n, Y = h.Normals[1], h.Normals[2], h.Variants
          for h, j in Y, nil, nil do
            if j.Kg < D.Kg then
              h = (j.Kg + D.Kg) + n.Kg
              local Y, V = C.Worth(x, nil, nil, h, j.Variant), (j.Value + D.Value) + n.Value
              if Y > V then
                table.insert(I, {Name = x, Variant = j.Variant, Parts = {j, D, n}, Kg = h, Hit = Y, Miss = C.Worth(x, nil, nil, h, nil), Inputs = V, Gain = Y - V})
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      table.sort(I, function(D, C)
        return D.Gain > C.Gain
      end)
      return I
    end
  end
end

local f13919 = function(I, I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    return function(I, C)
      local n, n = D.Treadmill()
      if not (n) then
        return nil
      end
      local D = Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
      local x, h, Y = I * D, C * D, n.Position * D
      C = h - x
      D = C.Magnitude
      if D < 1 then
        return nil
      end
      h = math.clamp((Y - x):Dot(C) / (D * D), 0, 1)
      local D, j = x + (C * h), (math.max(n.Size.X, n.Size.Z) / 2) + 6
      if (D - Y).Magnitude >= j then
        return nil
      elseif h <= 0 then
        return nil
      elseif h >= 1 then
        return nil
      end
      n = D - Y
      h = Y + ((if n.Magnitude < 0.1 then Vector3.new(-C.Z, 0, C.X) else n).Unit * (j + 4))
      return Vector3.new(h.X, I.Y, h.Z)
    end
  end
end

local f10714 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)

    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    return function()
      local I, J = D.Best()
      if not (I) then
        D.Note = "no rideable yet (Diamond Shop ride eggs)"
        C[4][C[6]]:Later("ride", 60)
        return
      end
      local b = n.Alive()
      if (if b then x.CarryCount() > 0 else not b) or h.InWindow(0) then
        D.Note = "waiting for empty hands"
        C[4][C[6]]:Later("ride", 3)
        return
      end
      if D.Mounted() then
        D.Tries = 0
        if Y:GetAttribute("SelectedRideable") == I then
          D.Note = ("riding %s (+%d%%)"):format(I, D.Percent(J))
          return
        end
        j.Fire(V.Remote("RideRemote"), "Dismount", nil)
        D.Note = "changing to " .. I
        C[4][C[6]]:Later("ride", 1)
        return
      end
      if D.Tries >= 3 then
        D.Tries = 0
        D.Note = "mount refused, retrying in a minute"
        C[4][C[6]]:Later("ride", 60)
        return
      end
      D.Tries = D.Tries + 1
      if Y:GetAttribute("HeldRideable") ~= I then
        b = j.Invoke(V.Remote("RideEggRemote"), 8, "Hold", I)
        if not b then
          D.Note = I .. " hold refused"
          C[4][C[6]]:Later("ride", 30)
          return
        end
      end
      j.Fire(V.Remote("RideRemote"), "Mount", I)
      D.Note = "getting on " .. I
      C[4][C[6]]:Later("ride", 2)
    end
  end
end

local f178 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    return function()
      if not (D.Hunting()) then
        return nil
      end
      local I = D.ZoneCache
      if I then
        if I.Version == D.Version then
          if I.Walk == C.WalkOnly() then
            if (os.clock() - I.At) < 30 then
              return I.Zone, I.Missing
            end
          end
        end
      end
      local n, x, h = {}, D.All, 0
      for Y, j in x() do
        if D.Map[Y] == nil then
          if C.ZoneWalkable(j.Zone) then
            n[j.Zone] = (n[j.Zone] or 0) + j.Chance
          end
          h += 1
        else
        end
      end
      x, I = -1
      for Y, j in n, nil, nil do
        if j > x then
          x, I = j, Y
        else
          if j == x then
            if Y < I then
              x, I = j, Y
            else
            end
          else
          end
        end
      end
      D.ZoneCache = {Version = D.Version, Zone = I, Missing = h, Walk = C.WalkOnly(), At = os.clock()}
      return I, h
    end
  end
end

          if x.Takeable(n, I, D) then
            local D, C = h.ZoneGuard(I.Zone), n:GetPivot().Position
            if D then
              if (D:GetPivot().Position - C).Magnitude < 350 then
                local I = (C - Y.Position).Magnitude
                if I < j then
                  j, V, J = I, n, D
                else
                end
              else
              end
            else
            end
          else
          end
        else
        end
      end
      return V, J
    end
  end
end

      if not (I) then
        C.Note = "no HTTP function for the server list"
        return
      end
      local D = game.PlaceId
      local J, b = pcall(I, {Url = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&excludeFullGames=true&limit=100"):format(D), Method = "GET"})
      I, D = ((J and (type(b) == "table")) and b.Body) or nil
      if type(I) == "string" then
        J, b = pcall(n.JSONDecode, n, I)
        D = if J then b else D
      else
      end
      b, I = (((type(D) == "table") and (type(D.data) == "table")) and D.data) or {}, {}
      for D, n in b, nil, nil do
        D = x.Number((type(n) == "table") and n.maxPlayers, 0) - x.Number((type(n) == "table") and n.playing, 0)
        if type(n) == "table" then
          if type(n.id) == "string" then
            if n.id ~= game.JobId then
              if not (C.Tried[n.id]) then
                if D >= 1 then
                  table.insert(I, n.id)
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      if #I == 0 then
        C.Note = "no server to hop to, retrying"
        h[4][h[6]]:Later("hop", 10)
        return
      end
      J = I[math.random(1, #I)]
      C.Tried[J] = true
      C.Target = J
      C.Count = C.Count + 1
      C.Note = "hopping to " .. J:sub(1, 8)
      Y(C.Note)

local f4775 = function(I, I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D)

    local C = I[2]

    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    return function()
      local I, j = D.Flags, C.Diamonds()
      if I.AutoPlotSkin == true then
        local D, V, J = C.Fences()
        if D then
          if V ~= D then
            n.Fire(x.Remote("PlotSkinRequest"), "Equip", D.Id)
            C.ShopNote = "equipped " .. D.Name
            h[4][h[6]]:Later("diamonds", 3)
            return
          end
        end
        if J then
          if (j >= J.Price) and ((os.clock() - (C.BoughtAt[J.Id] or -1000000000)) < 15) then
            C.ShopNote = J.Name .. " buy refused"
            h[4][h[6]]:Later("diamonds", 120)
            C.BoughtAt[J.Id] = nil
          elseif j >= J.Price then
            n.Fire(x.Remote("PlotSkinRequest"), "Buy", J.Id)
            C.BoughtAt[J.Id] = os.clock()
            C.ShopNote = "buying " .. J.Name
            h[4][h[6]]:Later("diamonds", 3)
          else
            C.ShopNote = ("saving for %s (%s / %s)"):format(J.Name, n.Short(j), n.Short(J.Price))
          end
          return
        end
      end
      if I.AutoRideEggs ~= true then
        C.ShopNote = "every fence owned"
        return
      end
      I = x.M.RideEgg
      local D, V = ((type(I) == "table") and (type(I.Prices) == "table")) and I.Prices.Mounted, math.huge
      I = ((type(D) == "table") and D) or {[1] = 1000}
      D = nil
      for J, b in I, nil, nil do
        b = n.Number(b, math.huge)
        if type(J) == "number" then
          V, D = (math.min(V, b)), if b <= j then if D then if J > D then J else D else J else D
        else
        end
      end
      if not D then
        C.ShopNote = ("waiting for diamonds (%s / %s)"):format(n.Short(j), n.Short(V))
        return
      end
      j = n.Invoke(x.Remote("RideEggRemote"), 15, "Hatch", "Mounted", D)
      if (type(j) == "table") and (#j > 0) then

        h[4][h[6]]:Wake("ride", 1)
      else
        C.ShopNote = "ride egg refused"
        h[4][h[6]]:Later("diamonds", 30)
      end
    end
  end

local f56 = function(I, I, I)

        return nil

        if Y then
          n = j
        end
        C = true
      end), os.clock() + (D or 5)
      while true do
        D = not C and (os.clock() < h)
        if not D then
          break
        end

      end

        return nil
      end
      return n
    end
  end
end

local f2610 = function(I, I, I, I, I, I)

    local n = I[3]
    return function(I, x)
      local x = D.Distance(I.Position)
      if x <= 4 then
        return true
      end
      if not (C.WalkOnly()) then
        if os.clock() >= (C.RagdollUntil - 0.2) then
          if n.CarryCount() == 0 then
            C.Ticket(I)
          end
        end
        if os.clock() < (C.RagdollUntil - 0.2) then
          x = D.Leap
          return x(I)
        end
        if C.V2() then
          if n.CarryCount() == 0 then
            if D.Distance(I.Position) <= D.HopRange then
              local C = D.Hop
              return C(I)
            end
          end
        end
      end
      x = D.Walk
      return x(I.Position, 3)
    end
  end
end

    local Y = I[6]
    return function(I)
      local j = D.Info[I]
      if j then
        return j
      end
      j = I:GetAttribute("AnimalName")
      if type(j) ~= "string" then
        return nil
      end
      local V, J = {Name = j}, I:GetAttribute("Rarity") or (C.M.EggRewards and n.Call(C.M.EggRewards.RarityOf, j))
      V.Rarity = J or "?"
      V.Size = I:GetAttribute("SizeMult")
      V.Kg = I:GetAttribute("WeightKg")
      V.Bracket = I:GetAttribute("WeightBracket") or ""
      V.Variant = I:GetAttribute("Variant")
      V.Mutation = I:GetAttribute("Mutation")
      V.Hatch = I:GetAttribute("HatchId")
      V.Zone = I:GetAttribute("ZoneId")
      V.Reserved = I:GetAttribute("ReservedUserId")
      V.Ends = n.Number(I:GetAttribute("DespawnAtServerTime"), 0)
      V.Seen = os.clock()
      V.Value = C.Worth(j, V.Size, V.Mutation, V.Kg, V.Variant)
      D.Info[I] = V
      if V.Hatch ~= nil then
        D.ByHatch[V.Hatch] = I
        j = x.Pending[V.Hatch]
        if j then
          if j.Mine then
            h.Mine[V.Hatch] = true
          end
        end
        if j then
          Y.Drop(j.Egg)
        end
        x.Pending[V.Hatch] = nil
      end
      return V
    end
  end
end

local f14270 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    local b = I[10]
    local K = I[11]
    local O = I[12]
    local L = I[13]
    return function(I)
      local W, e, G, m, d, z, i = D.Flags.StealOthers ~= false, C.FuseOnly(), n:GetServerTimeNow(), x.Floor(), -math.huge, math.huge, h.Live
      local n
      for x in i, nil, nil do
        local o = h.Read(x)
        if o then
          if h.Takeable(x, o, G) then
            if Y.Walkable(o) then
              if (((j.Must(o.Hatch) or V.Wants(o.Name)) or C.FuseWants(o.Name)) or J.Collect(x, o)) or b.Wants(o) then
                return {Model = x, Info = o, Ready = true}
              end
              local h = if e then not e else W or Y.Mine[o.Hatch]
              local j = h and (K.Match(o, m) or b.Upgrade(o, m))
              if j then
                h = O.Distance(x:GetPivot().Position)
                if (o.Value > d) or ((o.Value == d) and (h < z)) then
                  local j, J = {Model = x, Info = o, Ready = true}, o.Value
                  d, z, n = J, h, j
                else
                end
              else
              end
            else
            end
          else
          end
        else
        end
      end
      if I then
        return n
      end
      G = L.Pending
      for x, x in G, nil, nil do
        if (x.Hop or V.Wants(x.Name)) or C.FuseWants(x.Name) then
          if x.Egg.Parent then
            if (os.clock() - x.At) <= 20 then
              if Y.Walkable(x) then
                return {Pending = x, Info = x, Ready = false}
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      if e then
        return nil
      end
      G, e = D.Flags.WaitHatch == true, L.Pending
      for D, D in e, nil, nil do
        i = (((D.Mine and D.Egg.Parent) and ((os.clock() - D.At) <= 20)) and (O.Distance(D.Position) <= 40)) and ((G or K.Match(D, m)) or b.Upgrade(D, m))
        if i then
          if Y.Walkable(D) then
            return {Pending = D, Info = D, Ready = false}
          end
        end
      end
      G = L.Pending
      for D, C in G, nil, nil do
        if (os.clock() - C.At) > 20 then
          L.Pending[D] = nil
        else
          if C.Egg.Parent then
            e = (W or C.Mine) and (K.Match(C, m) or b.Upgrade(C, m))
            if e then
              if Y.Walkable(C) then
                I = O.Distance(C.Position)
                if (C.Value > d) or ((C.Value == d) and (I < z)) then
                  local x, h = {Pending = C, Info = C, Ready = false}, C.Value
                  d, z, n = h, I, x
                else
                end
              else
              end
            else
            end
          else
            L.Pending[D] = nil
          end
        end
      end
      return n
    end
  end
end

local f5780 = function(I, D, D)
  return function(D)
    local C = D[1]
    local n = D[2]
    local x = D[3]
    local h = D[4]
    local Y = D[5]
    return function()
      if C.Cache then
        if (os.clock() - C.CacheAt) < 2 then
          return C.Cache
        end
      end
      local D, j, V, J, b = n.Rate(), C.Standing()
      local K = {Status = "loading the board", Mine = D, Target = V or 0, Projected = D, Lines = {}, Top = {}, Seeds = {}, Plan = {}}
      if V then
        K.Status = (j and ("rank #%d"):format(j)) or "past #100"
        local O = math.min(10, #b)
        for L = 1, O, 1 do
          local W = b[L]
          local e, G, m, d, z = W.UserId == x.UserId, table.insert, K.Top, {}, "#" .. L
          local x, L = (e and {Text = "you", Color = "Accent"}) or tostring(W.Name), {Text = h.Short(h.Number(W.Value, 0)), Sort = h.Number(W.Value, 0)}
          I:lL(d, 0, z, x, L)
          G(m, d)
        end
        if J > 10 then
          O = b[J]
          local x, b, L, W, e, G = table.insert, K.Top, {}, {Text = "#" .. J, Color = "Accent"}, {Text = tostring(O.Name) .. " (target)", Color = "Accent"}, {}
          G.Text, G.Color = h.Short(h.Number(O.Value, 0)), "Accent"
          I:lL(L, 0, W, e, G)
          x(b, L)
        end
        if if j then j > 10 else not j then
          local x, J, b, L, W = table.insert, K.Top, {}, {Text = (j and ("#" .. j)) or "100+", Color = "Accent"}, {Text = h.Short(D), Color = "Accent"}
          I:lL(b, 0, L, {Text = "you", Color = "Accent"}, W)
          x(J, b)
        end
        O = C.Owned()
        local x, j, J = C.Seeds(O), C.Project(O)
        K.Projected = j
        local b, L, W = {}, 0, 0
        for e, e in x, nil, nil do
          if b[e.Name] then
          else
            b[e.Name] = true
            L += e.Gain
            W = math.max(W, C.Grow(e.Name, e.Kg))
            if #K.Seeds < 5 then
              local G, m, d, z, i, o, r, k = table.insert, K.Seeds, {}, e.Variant .. (" " .. e.Name), {Text = h.Short(e.Hit), Color = "Accent"}, h.Short(e.Miss), C.Time, C.Grow
              I:lL(d, 0, z, i, o, r(k(e.Name, e.Kg)))
              G(m, d)
            end
          end
        end
        O = V - D
        if O <= 0 then
          table.insert(K.Lines, "Target reached")
        else
          table.insert(K.Lines, "Gap: " .. C.Fmt(O))
          if J > 0 then
            table.insert(K.Lines, ("Eggs land: %s in %s"):format(C.Fmt(j), C.Time(J)))
          end
          b = math.max(J, W)
          if j >= V then
            table.insert(K.Lines, ("ETA: %s (your eggs)"):format(C.Time(J)))
          else
            if x[1] then
              table.insert(K.Lines, ("Seeds hit: %s in %s"):format(C.Fmt(j + L), C.Time(b)))
            end
            if x[1] and ((j + L) >= V) then
              table.insert(K.Lines, ("ETA: ~%s if the seeds hit (1 in 3 each)"):format(C.Time(b)))
            else
              local I = math.ceil(((V - j) - L) / ((x[1] and x[1].Gain) or 500000000000))
              table.insert(K.Lines, ("ETA: %s+ and ~%d more variant giant%s"):format(C.Time(b), I, ((I > 1) and "s") or ""))
            end
          end
        end
        b = {}
        for I in C.Stack() do
          table.insert(b, I)
        end
        table.sort(b)
        if #b > 0 then
          table.insert(K.Plan, "Takes Gold / Diamond: " .. table.concat(b, ", "))
        end
        table.insert(K.Plan, ("Takes any pet over %s (your pen's weakest), new ones too"):format(C.Fmt(Y.Floor())))
        if n.Power() < C.SpeedEnough then
          table.insert(K.Plan, "Under 25B speed: trains when idle")
        end
      end
      C.Cache, C.CacheAt = K, os.clock()
      return K
    end
  end
end

local f1396 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    return function()
      local I = D.Mine()
      if not (I) then
        C.Note = "no plot found"
        return false
      end
      if I:GetAttribute("TreadmillUnlocked") ~= true then
        if n.Cash() < 100 then
          C.Note = "treadmill locked, needs $100"
          return false
        end
        x.Fire("UnlockTreadmillRequest")
        h.WaitSignal(Y.Kick, 3)
      end
      local n, x = D.Treadmill()
      if not (x) then
        C.Note = "treadmill not found"
        return false
      end
      if j.Distance(x.Position) > 60 then
        I = D.Home()
        if I then
          if not (j.Go(I, "out")) then
            return false
          end
        else
          return false
        end
        task.wait(0.3)
      end
      C.Note = "walking onto the treadmill"
      C.Entering = true
      j.Drive(x.Position)
      I = os.clock() + 5
      while true do
        x = C.Active
        local D = (if x then not x else os.clock() < I) and not V.Unloaded
        if not D then
          break
        end
        J.WaitKick(I - os.clock())
      end
      j.Drive(nil)
      C.Entering = false
      if not (C.Active) then
        C.RetryAt = os.clock() + 15
        C.Note = "the treadmill did not start (" .. (tostring(n and n.Name) .. ")")
        return false
      end
      return true
    end
  end
end

local f15736 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    return function()
      if not (D.Ready) then
        return
      end
      local I = C.Picks()
      if not (I.Eggs) then
        if not (I.Sizes) then
          local D = I.Luck
          if D then
            if not (D > 0) then
              if not (I.Titanic) then
                C.Note = "pick eggs, sizes, a luck floor or Titanic"
                return
              end
            end
          elseif not (I.Titanic) then
            C.Note = "pick eggs, sizes, a luck floor or Titanic"
            return
          end
        end
      end
      I = (C.JoinedAt + 5) - os.clock()
      if I > 0 then
        C.Note = "checking this server"
        n[4][n[6]]:Later("hop", I)
        return
      end
      if os.clock() < C.Leaving then
        n[4][n[6]]:Later("hop", C.Leaving - os.clock())
        return
      end
      I = C.Found()
      if I then
        if C.Note ~= ("found: " .. I) then
          x("egg hop found: " .. I)
        end
        C.Staying = true
        C.Note = "found: " .. I
        return
      end
      if C.Busy() then
        C.Note = "taking our hatch, then hopping"
        n[4][n[6]]:Later("hop", 5)
        return
      end
      C.Staying = false
      C.Go()
    end
  end
end

      if type(j) == "string" then
        if j ~= "" then
          if V > h then
            local D = I and n.Call(I.Get, j)
            local J = ((type(D) == "table") and D.DisplayName) or j
            if (type(D) == "table") and D.Mutation then

            else
              table.insert(Y, ("%s: %s left"):format(J, x.Span(V - h)))
            end
          end
        end
      end
      V, j = x.Forecast(), {}
      for D, J in V, nil, nil do
        D = J.Info
        if D.Id then
          if not (j[D.Id]) then
            j[D.Id] = true
            table.insert(Y, ("Next %s: %s (in %s)"):format(tostring(D.DisplayName or D.Id), os.date("%H:%M", math.floor(J.At)), x.Span(J.At - h)))
          end

      end
      if #V == 0 then
        local D, V = C:GetAttribute("WeatherNextId"), n.Number(C:GetAttribute("WeatherNextAt"), 0)
        if type(D) == "string" then
          if D ~= "" then
            if V > h then
              j = I and n.Call(I.Get, D)
              table.insert(Y, ("Next: %s in %s"):format(((type(j) == "table") and j.DisplayName) or D, x.Span(V - h)))
            end
          end
        end
      end
      return ((#Y > 0) and table.concat(Y, "\n")) or "no weather scheduled"
    end
  end
end

          end
        else
          return nil
        end
      else
        return nil
      end
      local D, j = I.Size * 0.5, math.clamp(I.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(Y.Position).Z, -40, 40)
      n.Ray.FilterDescendantsInstances = {x.Character}
      for x = 0, 8, 1 do
        local V = I.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new((-D.X - 5) - (x * 3), 0, j))
        x = C:Raycast(Vector3.new(V.X, Y.Position.Y + 40, V.Z), Vector3.new(0, -120, 0), n.Ray)
        local I = Vector3.new(V.X, (x and (x.Position.Y + 3)) or Y.Position.Y, V.Z)
        if not (h.Bankable(I)) then
          return I
        end
      end
      return nil
    end
  end

local f7055 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    return function(I, C)
      local n = D.Registry()
      if n.Fns[I] then
        return true
      end
      if C then
        if type(hookfunction) ~= "function" then
          return false
        elseif type(clonefunction) ~= "function" then
          return false
        end
      else
        return false
      end
      local x, h = pcall(clonefunction, C)
      if x then
        if type(h) ~= "function" then
          return false
        end
      else
        return false
      end
      x = {Old = h}
      local h, Y = pcall(hookfunction, C, D.Wrap(I, x, n))
      if not (h) then
        return false
      end
      if type(Y) == "function" then
        x.Old = Y
      end
      n.Fns[I] = C
      return true
    end
  end
end

local f396 = function(I, D, D)
  return function(D, C, C)
    local C = D[1]
    local n = D[2]
    local x = D[3]
    local h = D[4]
    local Y = D[5]
    local j = D[6]
    local V = D[7]
    local J = D[8]
    local b = D[9]
    return function()
      local D = C.M.Pickaxe
      if D then
        if not (type(D.Tiers) == "table") then
          n.PickNote = "loading"
          return
        end
      else
        n.PickNote = "loading"
        return
      end
      local K, O = n.Pickaxes()
      local L, W, e, G = x.Cash(), D.Tiers
      for x, m in W, nil, nil do
        if x > O then
          if K[x] then
          else
            if type(m.Price) == "number" then
              local d = n.Refused["pickaxe" .. x]
              if (m.Price <= L) and not (d and (d > os.clock())) then
                e = if e then if x > e then x else e else x
              else
                G = if m.Price > L then if G then if x < G then x else G else x else G
              end
            else
            end
          end
        else
        end
      end
      if e then
        O = D.Tiers[e]
        C.Fire("PickaxeShopRequest", "Buy", e)
        h.WaitSignal(n.Kick, 3)
        if n.Pickaxes()[e] then
          Y(("bought %s (%s damage)"):format(tostring(O.Name), h.Short(O.Damage)))
          local x, Y, m, d, z = j.Push, {Kind = "upgrade", Title = tostring(O.Name), Text = "**Bought** the best pickaxe you can afford"}, {}, {}, h.Short
          I:lL(d, 0, "Damage", z(O.Damage))
          z, W = {}, h.Short
          I:lL(z, 0, "Price", "$" .. W(O.Price))
          I:lL(m, 0, d, z)
          Y.Extra = m
          x(Y)
          V[4][V[6]]:Wake("pickaxe", 1)
        else
          n.Refused["pickaxe" .. e] = os.clock() + 60
          n.PickNote = "refused: " .. tostring(O.Name)
          V[4][V[6]]:Wake("pickaxe", 1)
          return
        end
      end
      W, L = n.Pickaxes()
      if h.Number(J:GetAttribute("PickaxeTier"), 1) ~= L then
        C.Fire("PickaxeShopRequest", "Equip", L)
        h.WaitSignal(n.Kick, 2)
      end
      K = D.Tiers[L]
      if G and not e then
        b.Set(D.Tiers[G].Price, V[4][V[6]], "pickaxe")
        n.PickNote = ("next: %s at $%s"):format(tostring(D.Tiers[G].Name), h.Short(D.Tiers[G].Price))
      elseif e then
      else
        n.PickNote = "best pickaxe on: " .. tostring((K and K.Name) or L)
      end
    end
  end
end

local f7611 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    return function(I)
      while true do
        local n = D.Unloaded
        if n then
          break
        end
        n = I.Order
        for x, x in n, nil, nil do
          if D.Unloaded then
            break
          end
          if os.clock() >= x.Due then
            if select(2, pcall(x.Enabled)) == true then
              x.Due = os.clock() + x.Fallback
              I.Current = x.Name
              local D, h = pcall(x.Run)
              I.Current = ""
              if not (D) then
                C(x.Name .. (" failed: " .. tostring(h)))
              end
            end
          end
        end
        n = math.huge
        for D, D in I.Order, nil, nil do
          n = if select(2, pcall(D.Enabled)) == true then math.min(n, D.Due) else n
        end
        if n > os.clock() then
          local D = task.delay(math.clamp(n - os.clock(), 0.03, 30), I.Kick.Fire, I.Kick)
          I.Kick.Event:Wait()
          pcall(task.cancel, D)
        else
          task.wait()
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

local f3159 = function(I, D, D, D)
  return function(D)
    local C = D[1]
    local n = D[2]
    local x = D[3]
    local h = D[4]
    local Y = D[5]
    local j = D[6]
    local V = D[7]
    local J = D[8]
    local b = D[9]
    return function()
      local D = C.M.Trails
      if D then
        if not (D.Trails) then
          n.TrailNote = "loading"
          return
        end
      else
        n.TrailNote = "loading"
        return
      end
      local K, O = n.Owned(), n.BestOwned()
      local L, W, e, G, m = (O and O.Multiplier) or 1, x.Cash(), D.Trails
      for x, x in e, nil, nil do
        if K[x.Id] then
        else
          if x.Multiplier > L then
            if type(x.Price) == "number" then
              D = n.Refused["trail" .. x.Id]
              if (x.Price <= W) and not (D and (D > os.clock())) then
                G = if G then if x.Multiplier > G.Multiplier then x else G else x
              else
                m = if x.Price > W then if m then if x.Price < m.Price then x else m else x else m
              end
            else
            end
          else
          end
        end
      end
      if G then
        C.Fire("TrailShopRequest", "Buy", G.Id)
        h.WaitSignal(n.Kick, 3)
        if n.Owned()[G.Id] then
          Y(("bought %s (x%s)"):format(G.Name, h.Short(G.Multiplier)))
          L, e, W, D, K = j.Push, {Kind = "upgrade", Title = G.Name, Text = "**Bought** the best trail you can afford"}, {}, {}, h.Short
          I:lL(D, 0, "Speed", "x" .. K(G.Multiplier))
          local x, Y = {}, h.Short
          I:lL(x, 0, "Price", "$" .. Y(G.Price))
          I:lL(W, 0, D, x)
          e.Extra = W
          L(e)
          V[4][V[6]]:Wake("trails", 1)
        else
          n.Refused["trail" .. G.Id] = os.clock() + 60
          n.TrailNote = "refused: " .. G.Name
          V[4][V[6]]:Later("trails", 60)
          return
        end
      end
      O = n.BestOwned()
      if O then
        if h.Number(J:GetAttribute("EquippedTrail"), 0) ~= O.Id then
          C.Fire("TrailShopRequest", "Equip", O.Id)
          h.WaitSignal(n.Kick, 2)
        end
      end
      if m and not G then
        b.Set(m.Price, V[4][V[6]], "trails")
        n.TrailNote = ("next: %s at $%s"):format(m.Name, h.Short(m.Price))
      elseif G then
      else
        n.TrailNote = (O and ("best trail on: " .. O.Name)) or "no trail"
      end
    end
  end
end

local f4647 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    local b = I[10]
    local K = I[11]
    local O = I[12]
    local L = I[13]
    local W = I[14]
    return function()
      if not (D.Ready) then
        return
      end
      if not (C.Alive()) then
        n.Note = "waiting to respawn"
        return
      end
      if C.Roof() then
        n.Note = "stuck above the map: respawning"
        x[4][x[6]]:Later("move", 3)
        return
      end
      if h.On() then
        h.Step()
        return
      end
      if n.Decoy then
        if Y.CarryCount() > 0 then
          if n.On() then
            if not (n.WalkOnly()) then
              if j.Active then
                j.Leave()
              end
              n.Hold()
              x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
              return
            end
          end
        end
      end
      n.Decoy = nil
      if Y.CarryCount() > 0 then
        if j.Active then
          j.Leave()
        end
        if n.Carry() then
          x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
        else
          x[4][x[6]]:Later("move", 5)
        end
        return
      end
      if V.WantsPlacing() then
        if j.Active then
          j.Leave()
        end
        V.PlaceEggs()
        x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
        return
      end
      local I = n.On()
      if I then
        local D, h = Y.PetsFull()
        if D then
          n.Target, n.Note = "", ("pets full (%d): sell or place some"):format(h)
          if not (n.FullSent) then
            n.FullSent = true
            J.Push({Kind = "full", Title = ("Pets %d/%d"):format(h, h), Text = "**Bag full**: stealing paused until you sell or place some"})
          end
          I = false
        else
          n.FullSent = false
        end
      else
      end
      if I then
        local D = n.Best(false)
        if D then
          if D.Ready then
          else
            local h = n.Best(true)
            if h then
              D = h
            else
              if not ((b.Flags.WaitHatch == true) and D.Pending.Mine) then
                local h = K.EggToBreak()
                if h then
                  if C.Distance(h.Position) <= (C.Speed() * C.RespawnCost) then
                    if j.Active then
                      j.Leave()
                    end
                    n.Break(h)
                    x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
                    return
                  end
                end
              end
            end
          end
        else
        end
        if D then
          if j.Active then
            j.Leave()
          end
          n.Take(D)
          x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
          return
        end
        if n.WindowClosing() then
          if n.TryDecoy() then
            if j.Active then
              j.Leave()
            end
            n.Hold()
            x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
            return
          end
        end
        if (O.PickSet("BreakEggs") or L.HuntZone()) or V.FuseZone() then
          local D = W.Pick()
          if D then
            if j.Active then
              j.Leave()
            end
            n.Break(D)
            x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
            return
          end
        end
      end
      if j.On() then
        if os.clock() >= j.RetryAt then
          if I then
            n.Target = ""
            n.Note = "on the treadmill, the farm comes first"
          end
          if j.Hold() then
            x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move", 0.2)
            return
          end
        end
      end
      if I then
        local D = W.Pick()
        if D then
          n.Break(D)
          x[4][x[6]]:Wake("move")
          return
        end
        n.Target = ""
        n.Note = "nothing to break, waiting"
      end
      x[4][x[6]]:Later("move", ((n.On() and not I) and 5) or 15)
    end
  end
end

local f8601 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    return function(I, C, n)
      if n then
        if n.Parent then
          if I.X > -80 then
            return C
          end
        else
          return C
        end
      else
        return C
      end
      local x = Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
      local h, Y, j = I * x, C * x, n:GetPivot().Position * x
      x = Y - h
      Y = x.Magnitude
      if Y < 1 then
        return C
      end
      local V = (j - h):Dot(x) / Y
      if V < -6 then
        return C
      elseif V > math.min(Y, 140) then
        return C
      end
      Y = D.Hitboxes[n] or n:FindFirstChild("BodyHitbox", true)
      n = (((Y and Y:IsA("BasePart")) and (math.max(Y.Size.X, Y.Size.Z) / 2)) or 10) + 24
      if ((h + (x.Unit * math.max(V, 0))) - j).Magnitude >= n then
        return C
      end
      Y = ((h.Z >= j.Z) and 1) or -1
      Y = if math.abs(j.Z + (Y * n)) > 62 then -Y else Y
      return Vector3.new(j.X + (x.Unit.X * 12), I.Y, math.clamp(j.Z + (Y * n), -62, 62))
    end
  end
end

local f7287 = function(I, D, D, D)
  return function(D)
    local C = D[1]
    local n = D[2]
    local x = D[3]
    local h = D[4]
    local Y = D[5]
    local j = D[6]
    local V = D[7]
    local J = D[8]
    local b = D[9]
    return function()
      local D, K = C.Mine(), n.M.PlotUpgrade
      if D then
        if not (K) then
          x.PenNote = "loading"
          return
        end
      else
        x.PenNote = "loading"
        return
      end
      local C = h.Number(D:GetAttribute("PlotLevel"), 1)
      local O = h.Call(K.UpgradeCost, C)
      if type(O) ~= "number" then
        x.PenNote = "max level"
        return
      end
      K = x.Refused.pen
      local L = (Y.Cash() < O) or (K and (K > os.clock()))
      if L then
        j.Set(O, V[4][V[6]], "pen")
        x.PenNote = ("next: level %d at $%s"):format(C + 1, h.Short(O))
        return
      end
      n.Fire("UpgradePlotRequest")
      h.WaitSignal(x.Kick, 3)
      if h.Number(D:GetAttribute("PlotLevel"), 1) > C then
        x.PenNote = ("upgraded to level %d"):format(C + 1)
        J(("pen upgraded to level %d (%s slots)"):format(C + 1, tostring(D:GetAttribute("MaxAnimals"))))
        local n, Y, j, J, K, L = b.Push, {Kind = "upgrade", Title = ("Pen level %d"):format(C + 1), Text = "**Upgraded** your pen"}, {}, {"Slots", tostring(D:GetAttribute("MaxAnimals"))}, {}, h.Short(O)
        I:lL(K, 0, "Price", "$" .. L)
        I:lL(j, 0, J, K)
        Y.Extra = j
        n(Y)
        V[4][V[6]]:Wake("pen", 1)
      else
        x.Refused.pen = os.clock() + 60
        x.PenNote = "upgrade refused"
        V[4][V[6]]:Later("pen", 60)
      end
    end
  end
end

local f336 = function(I, I, I)

    return function()

      local D = (I and (type(I.ZoneEggs) == "table")) and I.ZoneEggs
      if not (D) then
        return
      end
      I = {}
      for C, C in n.Names, nil, nil do
        I[C] = true
      end
      for C = 1, 50, 1 do
        local x = D["Zone" .. C]
        if type(x) ~= "table" then
          break
        end
        local D = ((type(x.Eggs) == "table") and x.Eggs) or {}
        for x, x in D, nil, nil do
          if type(x) == "string" then
            n.ZoneOfType[x] = C
          end
          if type(x) == "string" then
            if not (I[x]) then
              I[x] = true
              table.insert(n.Names, x)
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

local f15157 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    return function()
      local I = D.M.Weather
      if type(I) ~= "table" then
        return C.Cast
      end
      local D, h, Y = n.Number(I.IntervalSeconds, 1800), x:GetAttribute("WeatherNextId"), n.Number(x:GetAttribute("WeatherNextAt"), 0)
      local j = math.floor(x:GetServerTimeNow() / D) + 1
      local x = j .. ("|" .. (tostring(h) .. ("|" .. Y)))
      if x == C.CastKey then
        return C.Cast
      end
      C.CastKey = x
      local V, J = {}, (Y > 0) and n.Call(I.ForSlot, math.floor((Y / D) + 0.001))
      if (type(J) == "table") and (J.Id == h) then
        for h = 0, 47, 1 do
          x = n.Call(I.ForSlot, j + h)
          if type(x) == "table" then
            local I, n = table.insert, {}
            n.At, n.Info = (j + h) * D, x
            I(V, n)
          end
        end
      end
      C.Cast = V
      return V
    end
  end
end

local f78 = function(I, I, I)

      if n < x then
        return 0
      end
      I = math.huge
      for D, D in C:GetChildren() do
        x = tonumber(D:GetAttribute("CashPerSecond"))
        I = if x then if x < I then x else I else I
      end
      return ((I == math.huge) and 0) or I
    end
  end
end

local f13058 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    return function(I)
      local C = D.Root()
      if not (C) then
        return math.huge
      end
      local D, n = I.Size, C.Position - I.Position
      local C, x, h = math.max(Vector3.new(n.X, 0, n.Z).Magnitude - (math.max(D.X, D.Z) / 2), 0), math.max(math.abs(n.Y) - (D.Y / 2), 0), I:GetAttribute("ReachBelow")
      x = if x > 0 then if n.Y < 0 then if type(h) == "number" then if h > 0 then math.max((-n.Y - (D.Y / 2)) - h, 0) else x else x else x else x
      return math.sqrt((C * C) + (x * x))
    end
  end
end

local f4711 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    return function()
      local I, j = D.Flags, C.BatKnown
      if (if j then not n.Known else not j) or ((os.clock() - C.BatAskedAt) > 300) then
        if (os.clock() - C.BatAskedAt) > 5 then
          C.BatAskedAt = os.clock()
          C.BatFire("Get")
          if I.AutoClaimBats == true then
            x.Fire("IndexRemote", "Get", nil)
          end
        end
        local D = C.BatKnown
        local x = if D then (I.AutoClaimBats == true) and not n.Known else not D
        if x then
          C.BatNote = "checking"
          h[4][h[6]]:Later("bats", 5)
          return
        end
      end
      j = C.BatTiers()
      if I.AutoClaimBats == true then
        for D, n in j, nil, nil do
          local x = (type(n) == "table") and n.Zone
          if type(x) == "number" then
            if not (C.BatOwned[D]) then
              if (os.clock() - (C.BatTried[x] or -60)) > 60 then
                if C.ZoneDone(x) then
                  C.BatTried[x] = os.clock()
                  C.BatFire("Claim", x)
                  C.BatNote = "claimed " .. tostring(n.Name)
                  Y(C.BatNote)
                  h[4][h[6]]:Later("bats", 2)
                  return
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      local D = 1
      for n in C.BatOwned, nil, nil do
        D = if n > D then if j[n] then n else D else D
      end
      local n = ((type(j[D]) == "table") and tostring(j[D].Name)) or "Bat"
      if I.AutoBestBat == true then
        if C.BatEquipped ~= D then
          C.BatFire("Equip", D)
          C.BatNote = "equipped " .. n
          Y(C.BatNote)
          h[4][h[6]]:Later("bats", 2)
          return
        end
      end
      I = 0
      for D in C.BatOwned, nil, nil do
        I += 1
      end
      C.BatNote = ("%d/%d bats, best bat on: %s"):format(I, math.max(#j, 1), n)
      h[4][h[6]]:Later("bats", 120)
    end
  end
end

local f2834 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D, D, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    return function()
      local I = D.Mine()
      local D = I and (I:FindFirstChild("Hitbox"))
      if not (D) then
        C.EggNote = "no plot found"
        return
      end
      I = Vector3.new(D.Position.X, D.Position.Y - (D.Size.Y / 2), D.Position.Z)
      if n.Distance(I) > 10 then
        if not (n.Go(CFrame.new(I + Vector3.new(0, 3, 6)), "near")) then
          return
        end
      end
      D, I = {}, C.Placed
      for J, J in I() do
        table.insert(D, {Position = J:GetPivot().Position, Height = C.PlacedHeight(J)})
      end
      local J, b = {}, C.EggTools
      for K, K in b() do
        table.insert(J, C.EggInfo(K))
      end
      table.sort(J, C.PlaceBefore)
      I = 0
      for K, O in J, nil, nil do
        local J, L, W = C.NewHeight(O.Tool:GetAttribute("AnimalName"), O.Kg), table.clone(D), false
        for e = 1, 4, 1 do
          e = C.Spot(L, J)
          if not e then
            break
          end
          table.insert(L, {Position = e, Height = J})
          if n.Distance(e) > 44 then
            if not (n.Go(CFrame.new(e + Vector3.new(3, 3, 0)), "near")) then
              return
            end
          end
          K, b = #C.Placed(), os.clock()
          x.Fire("MergeMachineRemote", "PlaceEgg", O.Tool, e)
          local n = os.clock() + 2
          while true do
            local x = (((#C.Placed() <= K) and (C.TooCloseAt < b)) and (os.clock() < n)) and not h.Unloaded
            if not x then
              break
            end
            Y.WaitSignal(C.Kick, n - os.clock())
          end
          if #C.Placed() > K then
            table.insert(D, {Position = e, Height = J})
            W = true
            break
          end
          if C.TooCloseAt >= b then
            C.Pad = math.min(C.Pad + 1, 6)
          end
        end
        if W then
          j(("placed %s ($%s/s, grows %s)"):format(O.Tool.Name, Y.Short(O.Value), Y.Clock(O.Grow)))
        else
          I += 1
        end
      end
      if I > 0 then
        C.PlaceRetryAt, C.PlaceWhen = os.clock() + 180, #C.Placed()
        C.EggNote = ("no room on the plot for %d egg%s: waiting for a hatch"):format(I, ((I == 1) and "") or "s")
      else
        C.PlaceWhen = -1
      end
      V[4][V[6]]:Wake("eggs", 1)
    end
  end
end

              Y, W = ((D < 128) and 9) or 25, D
            else
              local D, C = V[4][J], V[7]
              local n = D + 1
              D = I[54](C, n)
              Y, K, O, L = ((D < 128) and 11) or 8, C, n, D
            end
          elseif Y <= 24 then
            local D = I[54](G, b + 1)
            Y, e = ((D < 128) and 16) or 14, D
          else
            local D = I[54](G, b + 2)
            Y, e = ((D < 128) and 27) or 17, D
          end
        elseif Y <= 27 then
          if Y <= 26 then
            Y, L, O = 15, (((L - 128) * 128) + ((W - 128) * 16384)) + e, O + 3
          else
            Y, L, b = 21, (((L - 128) * 128) + ((W - 128) * 16384)) + e, b + 3
          end
        elseif Y <= 28 then
          local D, C, n = j[4], j[5], j[2]
          local x, h = D + C, C <= 0
          C, D = x >= n, x <= n
          n = (h and C) or (not h and D)
          j[4] = x
          if n then
            Y, z = 13, x
          else
            Y = 1
          end
        else
          local D, C = I[54](G, b), b + 1
          I[59](K, O + L, D, W)
          Y = 0
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

local f2931 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    return function()
      local I = D.FuseLocked()
      if I then
        if C.Flags.FarmFuse ~= true then
          D.FuseMissing = {}
          D.FuseNote = ("locked: needs %s speed power"):format(n.Short(I))
          x[4][x[6]]:Later("fuse", 120)
          return
        end
      end
      if not (D.FuseState and D.FuseRecipe) then
        h.Fire("FuseMachineRequest", "Sync")
        if not (D.FuseWait(3, "sync")) then
          D.FuseNote = "fuse machine not answering"
          x[4][x[6]]:Later("fuse", 30)
          return
        end
      end
      if #D.FuseNeeds() == 0 then
        D.FuseNote = "no recipe from the machine"
        x[4][x[6]]:Later("fuse", 60)
        return
      end
      local b, K, O, L = D.FuseHeld(), {}, {}, D.FuseNeeds
      for W, W in L() do
        if not (D.FuseState[W]) then
          if not (b[W]) then
            K[W] = true
            table.insert(O, W)
          end
        end
      end
      D.FuseMissing = K
      K = (I and ("locked (needs %s speed), "):format(n.Short(I))) or ""
      if #O > 0 then
        D.FuseNote = K .. ((((C.Flags.FarmFuse == true) and "farming ") or "needs ") .. table.concat(O, ", "))
        if C.Flags.FarmFuse == true then
          if not (D.FuseZone()) then
            D.FuseNote = D.FuseNote .. ": their guard outruns you on foot (TP (Risky) or more speed)"
          end
        end
        Y.Kick:Fire()
        j[4][j[6]]:Wake("move")
        return
      end
      if I then
        D.FuseNote = ("locked: all in hand, fuses at %s speed"):format(n.Short(I))
        x[4][x[6]]:Later("fuse", 120)
        return
      end
      if C.Flags.AutoFuse ~= true then
        D.FuseNote = "all in hand: turn on Auto Fuse"
        return
      end
      O = D.FuseNeeds
      for C, C in O() do
        if not (D.FuseState[C]) then
          h.Fire("FuseMachineRequest", "Deposit", C, b[C])
          if not (D.FuseWait(3, "in", C)) then
            D.FuseNote = "deposit refused: " .. C
            x[4][x[6]]:Later("fuse", 30)
            return
          end
        end
      end
      D.FuseFused = false
      h.Fire("FuseMachineRequest", "Fuse")
      if not (D.FuseWait(5, "fused")) then
        I = D.FuseState
        for C in I, nil, nil do
          h.Fire("FuseMachineRequest", "Return", C)
        end
        D.FuseNote = "fuse refused, pets returned"
        x[4][x[6]]:Later("fuse", 60)
        return
      end
      V.Fused = V.Fused + 1
      D.FuseNote = ("fused %d egg%s"):format(V.Fused, ((V.Fused == 1) and "") or "s")
      J("fused a Celestial Egg")
      D.FuseState = nil
      D.FuseRecipe = nil
      x[4][x[6]]:Wake("fuse", 1)
    end
  end
end

        else
          return
        end
      else
        return
      end
      n = nil
      for D, D in I:GetChildren() do
        if D:IsA("GuiButton") then
          if D.Name == "Pet" then
            if D.Visible then
              local I, x, h = n or C.PickIndex()
              for Y, j in D:GetChildren() do
                if j:IsA("TextLabel") then
                  Y = j:GetAttribute("FuseRole")
                  if Y == "Name" then
                    x = j
                  else
                    h = if Y == "Weight" then j else h
                  end
                else
                end
              end
              C.PickTile(D, x, h, D:FindFirstChild("PetDecal"), I)
              n = I
            else
            end
          else
          end
        else
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

local f1099 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    return function()
      local I = D.Mine()
      if not (I) then
        C.Note = "no plot yet"
        return
      end
      if #C.Animals() == 0 then
        C.Note = "nothing to place"
        C.Sig = C.Signature()
        return
      end
      if C.Sig == C.Signature() then
        C.Note = "pen holds your best"
        return
      end
      local D = (C.At + 2) - os.clock()
      if D > 0 then
        n[4][n[6]]:Later("place", D)
        return
      end
      C.At = os.clock()
      local D, J = x.Number(I:GetAttribute("CashPerSecond"), 0), x.Number(I:GetAttribute("AnimalsPlaced"), 0)
      h.Fire("PetsInventoryRemote", "EquipBest", nil)
      x.WaitSignal(Y.Kick, 1.5)
      task.wait(0.3)
      local h, Y = x.Number(I:GetAttribute("CashPerSecond"), 0), x.Number(I:GetAttribute("AnimalsPlaced"), 0)
      if Y > J then
        j.Placed = j.Placed + (Y - J)
      end
      if h > D then
        C.Gain = C.Gain + (h - D)
        V(("placed, pen +$%s/s"):format(x.Short(h - D)))
      end
      C.Sig = C.Signature()
      C.Note = ((h > D) and ("+$%s/s"):format(x.Short(h - D))) or "pen holds your best"
      n[4][n[6]]:Wake("sell", 1)
    end
  end
end

local f83 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I, D)
    local D = I[1]
    return function(I)
      I = tostring(I or ""):lower():gsub("/s$", ""):gsub("[%s,%$_]", "")
      if I == "" then
        return 0
      end
      local C = tonumber(I)
      if C then
        return math.max(0, C)
      end
      local n, x = I:match("^(%d*%.?%d+)(%a+)$")
      C = x and D.Suffix[x]
      if n then
        if not C then
          return 0
        end
      else
        return 0
      end
      return math.max(0, (tonumber(n) or 0) * C)
    end
  end
end

local f8218 = function(I, D, D, D, D)
  return function(D, C, C, C)
    local C = D[1]
    local n = D[2]
    local x = D[3]
    local h = D[4]
    return function(D, Y)
      local j = C.Amount(n.Flags.StealBelow)
      if j > 0 then
        if (D.Value or 0) >= j then
          local V = I[46](C.Short(j))
          return ("over Steal Below $%s/s"):format(I:uL(V))
        end
      end
      if x.MutantRule(D) then
        return nil
      end
      j = C.PickSet("Rarities")
      if j then
        if not (j[D.Rarity or ""]) then
          return tostring(D.Rarity) .. " is not in Pet Rarities"
        end
      end
      j = C.PickSet("Mutations")
      if j then
        if not (x.TraitIn(j, D)) then
          return "its mutation is not in Mutations"
        end
      end
      j = C.PickSet("Sizes")
      if j then
        if not (j[x.SizeOf[D.Bracket or ""] or "Normal"]) then
          return "its size is not in Sizes"
        end
      end
      j = C.Amount(n.Flags.StealAbove)
      if D.Value < j then
        local x = I[46](C.Short(j))
        return ("under Steal Above $%s/s"):format(I:uL(x))
      end
      Y = Y or h.Floor()
      if n.Flags.OnlyUpgrades ~= false then
        if D.Value <= Y then
          j = I[46](C.Short(Y))
          return ("under your pen's weakest $%s/s (Only Pen Upgrades)"):format(I:uL(j))
        end
      end
      return nil
    end
  end
end

local f13264 = function(I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    return function(I, n)
      if not (n.Eggs) then
        if not (n.Sizes) then
          local x = n.Luck
          if x then
            if not (x > 0) then
              return false
            end
          else
            return false
          end
        end
      end
      if n.Eggs then
        if not (n.Eggs[I:GetAttribute("EggType") or ""]) then
          return false
        end
      end
      if n.Sizes then
        if not (n.Sizes[D.SizeOf[I:GetAttribute("WeightBracket") or ""] or "Normal"]) then
          return false
        end
      end
      local D = n.Luck
      if D then
        if D > 0 then
          if C.Luck(I) < n.Luck then
            return false
          end
        end
      end
      return true
    end
  end
end

local f4583 = function(I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    return function(I)
      local x = D.PickSet("StealMutations")
      if not (x) then
        return false
      end
      local h, Y = C.Trait(I)
      if not ((h ~= nil) and x[h]) then
        if Y ~= nil then
          if not (x[Y]) then
            return false
          end
        else
          return false
        end
      end
      Y = D.PickSet("MutantRarities")
      if Y then

      if not (C) then
        return false, "no HTTP function"

      j.Body = x:JSONEncode(I:uL(V))

      if C then

          return false, "HTTP " .. C
        elseif C >= 300 then
          return false, "HTTP " .. C
        end
      end
      return true
    end
  end

local f402 = function(I, I, I, I, I)

    local n = I[3]

    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    local V = I[8]
    local J = I[9]
    return function()
      local I = D.Number(C:GetAttribute("MergeCooldownEndsAt"), 0) - n:GetServerTimeNow()
      if I > 0 then
        x[4][x[6]]:Later("seed", I + 0.2)
        return
      end
      I = h.Seeds(h.Owned())[1]
      if not I then
        h.ModeNote = "no seed yet: taking Gold / Diamond"
        x[4][x[6]]:Later("seed", 30)
        return
      end
      Y.Reply = nil
      j.Fire("MergeMachineRemote", "Merge", {I.Parts[1].Inst, I.Parts[2].Inst, I.Parts[3].Inst})
      local C = os.clock() + 4
      while true do
        local n = Y.Reply
        local j = (if n then not n else os.clock() < C) and not V.Unloaded
        if not j then
          break
        end
        D.WaitSignal(Y.Kick, C - os.clock())
      end
      if Y.Reply then
        h.ModeNote = ("seeded %s %s: 1 in 3 for %s"):format(I.Variant, I.Name, h.Fmt(I.Hit))
        J(h.ModeNote)
        x[4][x[6]]:Wake("eggs", 1)
        x[4][x[6]]:Wake("place", 2)
        h.CacheAt = 0
      else
        h.Refused[I.Name] = os.clock() + 120
        h.ModeNote = "the machine refused " .. I.Name
      end
      x[4][x[6]]:Later("seed", 5)
    end
  end
end

      end
    end
  end

      if type(I) == "string" then

          local h = C.Number(C.Call(x and x.EggFinalHeight, I, C.Number(n, 0)), 0)
          if h > 0 then
            return h / D
          end
        end
      end
      return 6
    end
  end
end

local f12378 = function(I, D, D, D)

          return C.InvIdx
        end
      end
      C.InvDirty = false
      local D, Y, j = {Full = {}, Label = {}}, {}, n:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
      I:lL(Y, 0, j, n.Character)
      local I, n = 0, 0
      for V, J in Y, nil, nil do
        for Y, b in J:GetChildren() do
          j = b:IsA("Tool") and x:HasTag(b, "MergeEggTool")
          Y = j or (b:IsA("Tool") and x:HasTag(b, "AnimalTool"))
          if Y then
            V = h.Worth(b:GetAttribute("AnimalName"), (if j then not j else b:GetAttribute("SizeMult")) or nil, b:GetAttribute("Mutation"), b:GetAttribute("WeightKg"), b:GetAttribute("Variant"))
            if V > 0 then
              if j and (b:GetAttribute("EggType") ~= nil) then
              else
                local x, h = C.ToolKey(b, j)
                D.Full[x .. ("|" .. h)] = V
                h = D.Label[x]
                D.Label[x] = (((h == nil) or (h == V)) and V) or false
                if j then
                  h = I + V
                  I = h
                else
                  x = n + V
                  n = x
                end
              end
            else
            end
          else
          end
        end
      end
      C.InvIdx = D
      C.InvPets = n
      C.InvEggs = I
      return D
    end
  end
end

local f1 = function(I, D, D, D)
  return function(D, C)
    local C = D[1]
    return function(D, n, ...)
      if not D then
        return nil, "remote missing"
      end
      local x, h, Y, j = table.pack(...), false
      local V, J = task.spawn(function()
        local b, K = pcall(function()
          local O, L = table.pack, I[46](table.unpack(x, 1, x.n))
          return O(D:InvokeServer(I:uL(L)))
        end)
        if b then
          Y = K
        else
          j = K
        end
        h = true
      end), os.clock() + (n or 8)
      while true do
        n = (not h and (os.clock() < J)) and not C.Unloaded
        if not n then
          break
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
      end
      if not h then
        pcall(task.cancel, V)
        return nil, "timeout"
      end
      if j then
        J = j
        return nil, J
      end
      V, J, n = table.unpack, Y, Y.n
      return V(J, 1, n)
    end
  end
end

local f8203 = function(I, I, I)

    local C = I[2]

      if h then
        if h.Name == "AnimalPickups" then
          if I:GetAttribute("Despawning") then
            return false
          end
        else
          return false
        end
      else
        return false
      end
      if n.Reserved ~= nil then
        if n.Reserved ~= D.UserId then
          return false
        end
      end
      if n.Ends > 0 then
        if (n.Ends - x) < 1.5 then
          return false
        end
      end
      x = C.Skip[I]
      return not (x and (x > os.clock()))
    end
  end
end

local f6369 = function(I, I, I, I, I)

      end

      D = os.clock() + C
      if D < n.Due then

      end

      end
    end

local f5124 = function(I, I, I, I, I, I)
  return function(I)
    local D = I[1]
    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    local x = I[4]
    local h = I[5]
    local Y = I[6]
    local j = I[7]
    return function()
      if not (D.On()) then
        return false
      end
      if C.CarryCount() > 0 then
        return true
      end
      local I = n.Pending
      for C, C in I, nil, nil do
        if (os.clock() - C.At) <= 20 then
          if C.Hop then
            return true
          elseif C.Mine then
            if x.Match(C) then
              return true
            end
          end
        end
      end
      local C, n = h:GetServerTimeNow(), Y.Live
      for h in n, nil, nil do
        I = Y.Read(h)
        if I then
          if I.Hatch ~= nil then
            if Y.Takeable(h, I, C) then
              if j.Must(I.Hatch) then
                return true
              elseif D.Mine[I.Hatch] then
                if x.Match(I) then
                  return true
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      return false
    end
  end
end

local f209 = function(I, I, I)

    local C = I[2]
    local n = I[3]
    return function()
      D.Stop()

        return
      elseif C.Unloaded then
        return
      end
      D.Running = true
      local I = D.Rendering()
      if I then
        local C, x = pcall(D.Read, I, "QualityLevel")
        if C then
          D.Quality = x
          pcall(D.Write, I, "QualityLevel", Enum.QualityLevel.Level01)
        end
      end
      I = n.Path(workspace, "Build", "ZoneBuilds")
      local C = (I and I:GetChildren()) or {}
      for n, n in C, nil, nil do
        D.Hide(n:FindFirstChild("Decor"))
      end
      I = game:GetService("Lighting")
      D.Set(I, "GlobalShadows", false)
      D.Hide(I:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere"))
      for C, C in I:GetDescendants() do
        D.Light(C)
      end
      table.insert(D.Connections, I.DescendantAdded:Connect(D.Light))
      I = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
      if I then
        D.Set(I, "Decoration", false)
        D.Set(I, "WaterWaveSize", 0)
        D.Set(I, "WaterWaveSpeed", 0)
        D.Set(I, "WaterReflectance", 0)
      end
      table.insert(D.Connections, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(D.Strip))
      task.spawn(D.Pass)
    end
  end
end
